```

terminal.js:
```
const accessToken = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('token') || '';
const headers = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'x-terminal-token': accessToken };
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
let connectedWallet = null;

async function api(path, opts = {}) {
  const r = await fetch(path, { ...opts, headers: { ...headers, ...(opts.headers || {}) } });
  const t = await r.text();
  let d; try { d = JSON.parse(t) } catch { d = t }
  if (!r.ok) throw d;
  return d;
}

function out(cell, msg, cls = '') {
  const box = cell.closest('.cell') ? cell.closest('.cell').querySelector('.cell-output') : (cell.querySelector ? cell.querySelector('.cell-output') : null);
  if (!box) return;
  box.className = 'cell-output ' + cls;
  box.textContent = typeof msg === 'string' ? msg : JSON.stringify(msg, null, 2);
}

function showPanel(id, btn) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
  $(id).classList.add('active');
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  if (btn) btn.classList.add('active');
  if (id === 'memoryPanel') searchMemory();
  if (id === 'receiptPanel') loadReceipts();
  if (id === 'processPanel') loadProcesses();
  if (id === 'appPanel') loadApps();
  if (id === 'tokenPanel') { loadLeaderboard(); loadLaunchedTokens(); }
  if (id === 'financePanel') loadFinance();
  if (id === 'claimosPanel') loadMyClaims();
  if (id === 'pixelatorPanel') loadPixelatorPage();
  if (id === 'crawlerPanel') loadCrawlerPage();
}

async function refreshStatus() {
  try {
    const j = await fetch('/status').then(r => r.json());
    $('connText').textContent = 'Connected';
    $('connPill').classList.add('live');
    $('up').textContent = j.uptime;
    $('cwd').textContent = j.runtime_cwd || j.cwd;
    $('kernel').textContent = j.kernel_running ? 'connected' : 'idle';
    $('kernelText').textContent = j.kernel_running ? 'Python 3 connected' : 'Python 3 idle';
    $('kernelPill').classList.toggle('live', j.kernel_running);
    $('appCount').textContent = j.apps || 0;
    $('receiptCount').textContent = j.receipts || 0;
    $('memCount').textContent = j.memories;
    $('procCount').textContent = j.background_processes;
    if (j.token_rewards) renderRewardGrid(j.token_rewards);
    if (connectedWallet) loadWalletInfo(connectedWallet.address);
  } catch (e) { $('connText').textContent = 'Offline'; }
}

function renderRewardGrid(rewards) {
  const grid = $('rewardGrid');
  if (!grid) return;
  grid.innerHTML = Object.entries(rewards).map(([k, v]) => {
    const name = k.replace(/_/g, ' ');
    return `<div class="reward-item"><div class="reward-name">${name}</div><div class="reward-amt">+${v}</div></div>`;
  }).join('');
}

async function runCell(btn) {
  const cell = btn.closest('.cell');
  const code = cell.querySelector('.cell-editor').value;
  out(cell, 'Running...');
  try {
    let j;
    const walletAddr = connectedWallet?.address;
    if (cell.dataset.kind === 'shell') j = await api('/execute', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ command: code, timeout: 120, wallet_address: walletAddr }) });
    else if (cell.dataset.kind === 'llm') j = await api('/llm', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ prompt: code, timeout: 120, wallet_address: walletAddr }) });
    else if (cell.dataset.kind === 'app') j = await api('/agent/build', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ prompt: code, run: true, timeout: 120, wallet_address: walletAddr }) });
    else j = await api('/kernel/execute', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ code, timeout: 120, wallet_address: walletAddr }) });
    out(cell, (j.stdout || j.response || '') + (j.stderr ? ('\n' + j.stderr) : '') || JSON.stringify(j, null, 2));
    if (j.app) { $('appPreview').src = j.app.proxy_url + '?token=' + encodeURIComponent(accessToken); await loadApps(); }
    await refreshStatus();
  } catch (e) { out(cell, e, 'err'); }
}

async function runAll() { for (const btn of document.querySelectorAll('.cell .cell-run')) await runCell(btn); }

function addCell(kind = 'kernel') {
  const app = kind === 'app', shell = kind === 'shell', llm = kind === 'llm';
  const label = app ? 'App builder' : shell ? 'Shell' : llm ? 'LLM' : 'Code';
  const code = app ? 'Create a Flask app with / returning a glassmorphism HTML page and /api/status JSON.' : shell ? 'pwd && python3 --version' : llm ? 'Summarize this runtime and suggest next actions.' : 'print("new cell")';
  const mode = app ? 'LLM → backend' : shell ? 'bash' : llm ? 'OpenAI' : 'Python';
  const num = document.querySelectorAll('.cell').length + 1;
  const html = `<div class="cell" data-kind="${app ? 'app' : shell ? 'shell' : llm ? 'llm' : 'kernel'}"><div class="cell-gutter"><button class="cell-run" onclick="runCell(this)">▶</button><div class="cell-num">${num}</div></div><div class="cell-body"><div class="cell-meta">${label} <code>${mode}</code></div><textarea class="cell-editor">${code}</textarea><div class="cell-output empty">New cell ready.</div></div></div>`;
  $('cells').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
}

async function runNotebook() {
  const cells = [...document.querySelectorAll('.cell[data-kind="kernel"] .cell-editor')].map(t => t.value);
  const box = document.querySelector('.cell:last-child');
  try {
    const j = await api('/notebook/run', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ cells, timeout: 120, wallet_address: connectedWallet?.address }) });
    out(box, j); await refreshStatus();
  } catch (e) { out(box, e, 'err'); }
}

async function restartKernel() { try { await api('/kernel/restart', { method: 'POST', body: '{}' }); await refreshStatus(); } catch (e) { alert(JSON.stringify(e)); } }

async function runQuickShell() {
  try {
    const j = await api('/execute', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ command: $('quickCmd').value, timeout: 120, wallet_address: connectedWallet?.address }) });
    $('quickOut').textContent = (j.stdout || '') + (j.stderr ? ('\n' + j.stderr) : '');
  } catch (e) { $('quickOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function launchBg() {
  try {
    await api('/execute/background', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ command: $('bgCmd').value, timeout: 120 }) });
    setTimeout(loadProcesses, 700); await refreshStatus();
  } catch (e) { $('processList').textContent = JSON.stringify(e); }
}

async function loadProcesses() {
  try {
    const j = await api('/processes');
    $('processList').innerHTML = j.processes.map(p => `<div class="card"><h4>${p.status} ${p.process_id}</h4><div class="hint">${p.command}</div><pre>${(p.stdout || '') + (p.stderr || '')}</pre></div>`).join('') || '<p class="hint">No processes yet.</p>';
    await refreshStatus();
  } catch (e) { $('processList').textContent = JSON.stringify(e); }
}

async function loadApps() {
  try {
    const j = await api('/apps');
    $('appList').innerHTML = j.apps.map(a => `<div class="card"><h4>${a.status} ${a.name}</h4><div class="hint">${a.app_id} · port ${a.port}</div><div class="hint">${a.proxy_url}</div><div class="btn-row"><button class="btn btn-good" onclick="startApp('${a.app_id}')">Start</button><button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="stopApp('${a.app_id}')">Stop</button><button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="previewApp('${a.app_id}')">Preview</button></div><pre>${(a.stdout || '') + (a.stderr ? ('\n' + a.stderr) : '')}</pre></div>`).join('') || '<p class="hint">No generated apps yet.</p>';
    await refreshStatus();
  } catch (e) { $('appList').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function buildApp() {
  try {
    $('appOut').textContent = 'Building...';
    const j = await api('/agent/build', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ prompt: $('appPrompt').value, run: true, timeout: 120, wallet_address: connectedWallet?.address }) });
    $('appOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
    $('appPreview').src = j.app.proxy_url + '?token=' + encodeURIComponent(accessToken);
    await loadApps();
  } catch (e) { $('appOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function createSampleApp() {
  const content = `import os\nfrom flask import Flask, jsonify, request\napp = Flask(__name__)\n@app.route('/')\ndef home():\n    return '''<html><body style="margin:0;font-family:system-ui;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#111827,#7c3aed);color:white;display:grid;place-items:center;min-height:100vh"><main style="max-width:760px;padding:48px"><h1>HF VM app is live</h1><p>This backend is running inside the Space.</p><form method="post" action="/echo"><input name="text" placeholder="type something" style="padding:12px;border-radius:10px;border:0"><button style="padding:12px 16px;border-radius:10px;border:0">Echo</button></form><p><a style="color:#67e8f9" href="/api/hello">/api/hello</a></p></main></body></html>'''\n@app.route('/api/hello')\ndef hello():\n    return jsonify(message='hello from generated app', port=os.environ.get('PORT'))\n@app.route('/echo', methods=['POST'])\ndef echo():\n    return jsonify(echo=request.form.get('text',''))\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    app.run(host='0.0.0.0', port=int(os.environ.get('PORT', 9000)))\n`;
  try {
    const j = await api('/apps', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ name: 'sample-flask-vm-app', description: 'Sample live backend app', start_command: 'python3 app.py', files: [{ path: 'app.py', content }] }) });
    await api(`/apps/${j.app_id}/start`, { method: 'POST', body: '{}' });
    $('appOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
    $('appPreview').src = j.proxy_url + '?token=' + encodeURIComponent(accessToken);
    await loadApps();
  } catch (e) { $('appOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function startApp(id) { try { $('appOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(await api(`/apps/${id}/start`, { method: 'POST', body: '{}' }), null, 2); setTimeout(loadApps, 800); } catch (e) { $('appOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); } }
async function stopApp(id) { try { $('appOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(await api(`/apps/${id}/stop`, { method: 'POST', body: '{}' }), null, 2); await loadApps(); } catch (e) { $('appOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); } }
function previewApp(id) { $('appPreview').src = `/apps/${id}/proxy/?token=${encodeURIComponent(accessToken)}`; }

async function saveManualMemory() {
  try {
    await api('/memory', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ title: 'UI note', content: $('memContent').value, tags: ['ui'], importance: .8 }) });
    $('memContent').value = ''; await searchMemory(); await refreshStatus();
  } catch (e) { $('memoryResults').textContent = JSON.stringify(e); }
}

async function searchMemory() {
  try {
    const j = await api('/memory/search', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ query: $('memQuery').value, limit: 10 }) });
    $('memoryResults').innerHTML = j.memories.map(m => `<div class="card"><h4>${m.title}</h4><div class="hint">${m.source} score ${m.score ?? ''}</div><pre>${m.content}</pre></div>`).join('') || '<p class="hint">No matching memories.</p>';
  } catch (e) { $('memoryResults').textContent = JSON.stringify(e); }
}

async function loadReceipts() {
  try {
    const j = await api('/receipts?limit=20');
    renderReceipts(j.receipts);
  } catch (e) { $('receiptResults').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function searchReceipts() {
  try {
    const j = await api('/receipts/search', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ query: $('receiptQuery').value, limit: 20 }) });
    renderReceipts(j.receipts);
  } catch (e) { $('receiptResults').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

function renderReceipts(items) {
  $('receiptResults').innerHTML = (items || []).map(r => `<div class="card"><h4>${r.kind} · ${r.status}</h4><div class="hint">${r.receipt_id}</div><div class="hint">${r.title || ''}</div><pre>${JSON.stringify({ prompt_hash: r.prompt_hash, files_hash: r.files_hash, model: r.model, command: r.command, created_at: r.created_at }, null, 2)}</pre></div>`).join('') || '<p class="hint">No receipts yet.</p>';
}

async function deployVercel() {
  try { $('deployOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(await api('/deploy/vercel', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ project_dir: $('deployDir').value, prod: true }) }), null, 2); setTimeout(loadProcesses, 1000); }
  catch (e) { $('deployOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function deployNetlify() {
  try { $('deployOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(await api('/deploy/netlify', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ project_dir: $('deployDir').value, publish_dir: $('publishDir').value, prod: true }) }), null, 2); setTimeout(loadProcesses, 1000); }
  catch (e) { $('deployOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

function parseEnv() {
  const env = {};
  ($('setEnv').value || '').split('\n').forEach(line => { const i = line.indexOf('='); if (i > 0) env[line.slice(0, i).trim()] = line.slice(i + 1).trim() });
  return env;
}

function toggleKey(id) { const el = $(id); el.type = el.type === 'password' ? 'text' : 'password'; }
function useGroq() { $('providerSelect').value = 'groq'; $('setLlmBase').value = 'https://api.groq.com/openai/v1'; $('setLlmModel').value = 'llama-3.1-8b-instant'; }
function useOpenRouter() { $('providerSelect').value = 'openrouter'; $('setLlmBase').value = 'https://openrouter.ai/api/v1'; $('setLlmModel').value = 'openai/gpt-4o-mini'; }
function applyProvider() { if ($('providerSelect').value === 'groq') useGroq(); if ($('providerSelect').value === 'openrouter') useOpenRouter(); }

async function loadSettings() {
  try {
    const j = await api('/settings');
    $('setCwd').value = j.cwd || '/app';
    $('setTimeout').value = j.max_timeout || 600;
    $('setLlmBase').value = (j.llm || {}).api_base || 'https://api.groq.com/openai/v1';
    $('setLlmModel').value = (j.llm || {}).model || 'llama-3.1-8b-instant';
    $('settingsOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
  } catch (e) { $('settingsOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function saveSettings() {
  try {
    const env = parseEnv();
    if ($('groqKey').value) { env.GROQ_API_KEY = $('groqKey').value; env.LLM_API_KEY = $('groqKey').value; }
    if ($('openrouterKey').value) { env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY = $('openrouterKey').value; if ($('providerSelect').value === 'openrouter') env.LLM_API_KEY = $('openrouterKey').value; }
    if ($('hfToken').value) env.HUGGING_FACE_TOKEN = $('hfToken').value;
    if ($('vercelToken').value) env.VERCEL_TOKEN = $('vercelToken').value;
    if ($('netlifyToken').value) env.NETLIFY_AUTH_TOKEN = $('netlifyToken').value;
    const body = { cwd: $('setCwd').value, max_timeout: Number($('setTimeout').value || 600), env, llm: { api_base: $('setLlmBase').value, model: $('setLlmModel').value } };
    $('settingsOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(await api('/settings', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) }), null, 2);
    await refreshStatus();
  } catch (e) { $('settingsOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function testLLM() {
  try { await saveSettings(); $('settingsOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(await api('/llm', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ prompt: 'Reply with exactly: llm-ok', max_tokens: 20, timeout: 60 }) }), null, 2); }
  catch (e) { $('settingsOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

// Wallet & Token Economy
function openWalletModal() { $('walletModal').classList.add('open'); }
function closeWalletModal() { $('walletModal').classList.remove('open'); }

async function connectMetaMask() {
  closeWalletModal();
  try {
    if (!window.ethereum) { alert('MetaMask not detected. Please install MetaMask.'); return; }
    const accounts = await window.ethereum.request({ method: 'eth_requestAccounts' });
    const address = accounts[0];
    await finishWalletConnect(address, 'metamask');
  } catch (e) { alert('MetaMask connection failed: ' + (e.message || e)); }
}

async function connectPhantom() {
  closeWalletModal();
  try {
    const provider = window.solana || window.phantom?.solana;
    if (!provider) { alert('Phantom not detected. Please install Phantom.'); return; }
    const resp = await provider.connect();
    const address = resp.publicKey.toString();
    await finishWalletConnect(address, 'phantom');
  } catch (e) { alert('Phantom connection failed: ' + (e.message || e)); }
}

async function finishWalletConnect(address, provider) {
  try {
    const nonceRes = await api('/wallet/nonce');
    const message = nonceRes.message;
    let signature;
    if (provider === 'metamask') {
      signature = await window.ethereum.request({ method: 'personal_sign', params: [message, address] });
    } else {
      const provider2 = window.solana || window.phantom?.solana;
      const encoded = new TextEncoder().encode(message);
      const signed = await provider2.signMessage(encoded, 'utf8');
      signature = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(signed.signature)));
    }
    const j = await api('/wallet/connect', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ address, provider, signature, nonce: nonceRes.nonce }) });
    connectedWallet = { address: j.wallet, provider: j.provider, balance: j.balance };
    updateWalletUI();
    await refreshStatus();
  } catch (e) { alert('Wallet verification failed: ' + JSON.stringify(e)); }
}

function updateWalletUI() {
  if (!connectedWallet) return;
  $('walletLabel').textContent = connectedWallet.address.slice(0, 6) + '...' + connectedWallet.address.slice(-4);
  $('walletBtn').classList.add('btn-primary');
  $('tokenBalance').textContent = (connectedWallet.balance ?? 0).toLocaleString();
  $('tokenWallet').textContent = connectedWallet.address.slice(0, 10) + '...';
  $('walletRailCard').style.display = 'block';
  $('railWalletAddr').textContent = connectedWallet.address.slice(0, 10) + '...';
  $('railWalletBal').textContent = (connectedWallet.balance ?? 0).toLocaleString();
  $('railWalletProv').textContent = connectedWallet.provider;
  $('tokenActions').innerHTML = `<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="openWalletModal()">Switch Wallet</button>`;
}

async function loadWalletInfo(address) {
  try {
    const j = await api('/wallet/' + encodeURIComponent(address));
    if (connectedWallet && connectedWallet.address === j.wallet) {
      connectedWallet.balance = j.balance;
      updateWalletUI();
    }
  } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
}

async function loadLeaderboard() {
  try {
    const j = await api('/tokens/leaderboard');
    const rows = (j.leaderboard || []).slice(0, 10);
    $('leaderboardResults').innerHTML = `<table style="width:100%;font-size:12px;border-collapse:collapse"><thead><tr style="color:var(--text-3)"><th style="text-align:left;padding:6px 0">Rank</th><th style="text-align:left;padding:6px 0">Wallet</th><th style="text-align:right;padding:6px 0">Balance</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map((r, i) => `<tr style="border-top:1px solid var(--border)"><td style="padding:6px 0">${i + 1}</td><td style="padding:6px 0;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:11px">${r.wallet.slice(0, 10)}...</td><td style="padding:6px 0;text-align:right;font-weight:600">${r.balance.toLocaleString()}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>`;
    if (connectedWallet) {
      const me = rows.find(r => r.wallet.toLowerCase() === connectedWallet.address.toLowerCase());
      $('tokenRank').textContent = me ? '#' + (rows.indexOf(me) + 1) : '—';
    }
  } catch (e) { $('leaderboardResults').innerHTML = '<p class="hint">Leaderboard unavailable.</p>'; }
}

async function launchToken() {
  if (!connectedWallet) {
    alert('Connect a Solana wallet (Phantom) first.');
    return;
  }
  const name = $('launchName').value.trim();
  const symbol = $('launchSymbol').value.trim().toUpperCase();
  const decimals = parseInt($('launchDecimals').value) || 9;
  const supply = parseInt($('launchSupply').value) || 1000000000;
  if (!name || !symbol) { alert('Name and symbol required'); return; }
  $('launchOut').textContent = 'Launching token on Solana...';
  try {
    const j = await api('/tokens/launch', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ owner_address: connectedWallet.address, name, symbol, decimals, supply })
    });
    $('launchOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
    await loadWalletInfo(connectedWallet.address);
    await loadLaunchedTokens();
  } catch (e) {
    $('launchOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2);
  }
}

async function loadLaunchedTokens() {
  if (!connectedWallet) {
    $('launchedTokensResults').innerHTML = '<p class="hint">Connect wallet to view launched tokens.</p>';
    return;
  }
  try {
    const j = await api('/tokens/launched?wallet=' + encodeURIComponent(connectedWallet.address));
    const rows = j.tokens || [];
    $('launchedTokensResults').innerHTML = rows.map(t => `<div class="card"><h4>${t.symbol} — ${t.name}</h4><div class="hint">Mint: ${t.mint_address}</div><div class="hint">Network: ${t.network} · Supply: ${t.supply.toLocaleString()} · Decimals: ${t.decimals}</div><div class="hint">Launched: ${t.created_at}</div></div>`).join('') || '<p class="hint">No launched tokens yet.</p>';
  } catch (e) { $('launchedTokensResults').innerHTML = '<p class="hint">Failed to load.</p>'; }
}

async function loadFinance() {
  try {
    const j = await api('/finance/collateral');
    $('financeRevenue').textContent = (j.total_revenue_cents || 0).toLocaleString();
    $('financeDeferred').textContent = (j.total_deferred_cents || 0).toLocaleString();
    $('financeRealized').textContent = (j.total_realized_cents || 0).toLocaleString();
    $('tokenVelocity').textContent = (j.token_velocity || 0).toFixed(2);

    const rev = await api('/finance/revenue');
    $('financePeriods').innerHTML = (rev.periods || []).map(p => `<div class="stat-row"><span class="stat-label">${p.period}</span><span class="stat-value">${p.total_cents}¢</span></div>`).join('') || '<p class="hint">No period data.</p>';
    $('financeSources').innerHTML = (rev.sources || []).map(s => `<div class="stat-row"><span class="stat-label">${s.source}</span><span class="stat-value">${s.total_cents}¢</span></div>`).join('') || '<p class="hint">No source data.</p>';
  } catch (e) { $('financeRevenue').textContent = '—'; $('financeDeferred').textContent = '—'; $('financeRealized').textContent = '—'; }
}

async function reconcileFinance() {
  try {
    const j = await api('/finance/reconcile', { method: 'POST', body: '{}' });
    alert('Reconciliation complete. ' + JSON.stringify(j));
    await loadFinance();
  } catch (e) { alert('Reconciliation failed: ' + JSON.stringify(e)); }
}

async function evaluateClaim() {
  const title = $('claimTitle').value.trim();
  const desc = $('claimDesc').value.trim();
  const wallet = $('claimWallet').value.trim() || (connectedWallet ? connectedWallet.address : '');
  if (!title) { alert('Title required'); return; }
  $('claimOut').textContent = 'Evaluating claim...';
  try {
    const j = await api('/claimos/evaluate', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ title, description: desc, wallet, evidence: [] })
    });
    $('claimOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
    $('claimId').value = j.claim_id;
    if (j.probability_state) {
      $('claimP').textContent = (j.probability_state.p_recovery * 100).toFixed(2) + '%';
    }
    await loadMyClaims();
  } catch (e) { $('claimOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function getClaim() {
  const id = $('claimId').value.trim();
  if (!id) { alert('Enter a claim ID'); return; }
  $('claimOut').textContent = 'Loading claim...';
  try {
    const j = await api('/claimos/' + encodeURIComponent(id));
    $('claimOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
    if (j.claim) {
      $('claimP').textContent = ((j.claim.p_recovery || 0) * 100).toFixed(2) + '%';
      $('claimLiq').textContent = ((j.claim.liquidity_score || 0) * 100).toFixed(2) + '%';
      $('claimCx').textContent = (j.contradictions || []).length;
    }
  } catch (e) { $('claimOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function scanContradictions() {
  const id = $('claimId').value.trim();
  const wallet = connectedWallet ? connectedWallet.address : '';
  if (!id) { alert('Enter a claim ID'); return; }

    const j = await api('/claimos/' + encodeURIComponent(id) + '/contradictions', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ wallet })
    });
    $('claimOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
    $('claimCx').textContent = j.contradiction_count;
  } catch (e) { $('claimOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function loadMyClaims() {

async function pixelateHtml() {
  const websiteId = $('pxWebsiteId').value.trim() || 'demo-site';
  const url = $('pxUrl').value.trim() || 'about:blank';
  const title = $('pxTitle').value.trim();
  const html = $('pxHtml').value;
  const wallet = $('pxWallet').value.trim() || (connectedWallet ? connectedWallet.address : '');
  if (!html) { alert('Paste HTML content first.'); return; }
  $('pxOut').textContent = 'Pixelating HTML into glyph units...';
  try {
    const j = await api('/pixelator/ingest', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ html, url, website_id: websiteId, title, wallet })
    });
    $('pxOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
    $('pxPageId').value = j.page_id;
    $('pxActivation').textContent = (j.page_activation_value || 0).toFixed(2);
    $('pxGlyphCount').textContent = j.glyph_count.toLocaleString();
    if (j.tokens_debited) { await loadWalletInfo(wallet); }
  } catch (e) { $('pxOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function getPageGlyphs() {
  const pageId = $('pxPageId').value.trim();
  if (!pageId) { alert('Enter a Page ID'); return; }
  $('pxGlyphOut').textContent = 'Loading glyphs...';
  try {
    const j = await api('/pixelator/page/' + encodeURIComponent(pageId) + '/glyphs?limit=100');
    const glyphs = j.glyphs || [];
    $('pxGlyphOut').textContent = 'Glyphs: ' + glyphs.length + '\n' + JSON.stringify(glyphs.slice(0, 5), null, 2) + '\n... (showing first 5)';
  } catch (e) { $('pxGlyphOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function getPageActivation() {
  const pageId = $('pxPageId').value.trim();
  if (!pageId) { alert('Enter a Page ID'); return; }
  $('pxGlyphOut').textContent = 'Loading activation...';
  try {
    const j = await api('/pixelator/page/' + encodeURIComponent(pageId) + '/activation');
    $('pxGlyphOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
    if (j.page) {
      $('pxActivation').textContent = (j.page.page_activation_value || 0).toFixed(2);
      $('pxGlyphCount').textContent = j.glyph_count.toLocaleString();
      $('pxTopValue').textContent = (j.max_glyph_value || 0).toFixed(2);
    }
  } catch (e) { $('pxGlyphOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function getTopGlyphs() {
  const websiteId = $('pxWebsiteId').value.trim() || 'demo-site';
  $('pxGlyphOut').textContent = 'Loading top glyphs...';
  try {
    const j = await api('/pixelator/website/' + encodeURIComponent(websiteId) + '/top-glyphs?limit=20');
    const glyphs = j.glyphs || [];
    $('pxGlyphOut').textContent = 'Top ' + glyphs.length + ' glyphs:\n' + JSON.stringify(glyphs.slice(0, 10), null, 2);
    if (glyphs.length > 0) {
      $('pxTopValue').textContent = (glyphs[0].glyph_value || 0).toFixed(2);
    }
  } catch (e) { $('pxGlyphOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function loadPixelatorPage() {
  if (connectedWallet) {
    $('pxWallet').value = connectedWallet.address;
  }
  $('pxOut').textContent = 'Ready. Paste HTML and click Pixelate.';
}

async function addCrawlerTarget() {
  const websiteId = $('crWebsiteId').value.trim();
  const rootUrl = $('crRootUrl').value.trim();
  const name = $('crName').value.trim();
  const depth = parseInt($('crDepth').value) || 2;
  const priority = parseFloat($('crPriority').value) || 1.0;
  if (!websiteId || !rootUrl) { alert('website_id and root_url required'); return; }
  $('crOut').textContent = 'Adding target...';
  try {
    const j = await api('/crawler/targets', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ website_id: websiteId, root_url: rootUrl, name, crawl_depth: depth, priority })
    });
    $('crOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
    $('crTargetId').value = j.target_id;
    await loadCrawlerTargets();
  } catch (e) { $('crOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function loadCrawlerTargets() {
  try {
    const j = await api('/crawler/targets');
    $('crOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
    $('crTargetCount').textContent = j.count;
  } catch (e) { $('crOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function enqueueCrawl() {
  const targetId = $('crTargetId').value.trim();
  const url = $('crUrl').value.trim();
  if (!targetId || !url) { alert('target_id and url required'); return; }
  $('crActionOut').textContent = 'Enqueuing...';
  try {
    const j = await api('/crawler/queue', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ target_id: targetId, url })
    });
    $('crActionOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
    await loadCrawlerQueue();
  } catch (e) { $('crActionOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function loadCrawlerQueue() {
  try {
    const j = await api('/crawler/queue?limit=50');
    $('crActionOut').textContent = 'Queue (' + j.count + ' items):\n' + JSON.stringify(j.queue.slice(0, 5), null, 2) + '\n... (showing first 5)';
    const pending = j.queue.filter(q => q.status === 'pending').length;
    $('crQueueCount').textContent = pending;
  } catch (e) { $('crActionOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function runCrawlStep() {
  $('crActionOut').textContent = 'Running RL crawl step...';
  try {
    const j = await api('/crawler/ingest', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({}) });
    $('crActionOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
    if (j.status === 'completed') {
      await loadCrawlerResults();
      await loadCrawlerPolicy();
    }
  } catch (e) { $('crActionOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function loadCrawlerResults() {
  try {
    const j = await api('/crawler/results?limit=20');
    $('crActionOut').textContent = 'Results (' + j.count + '):\n' + JSON.stringify(j.results.slice(0, 5), null, 2) + '\n... (showing first 5)';
  } catch (e) { $('crActionOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function evolveCrawler() {
  $('crActionOut').textContent = 'Running GA evolution...';
  try {
    const j = await api('/crawler/evolve', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({}) });
    $('crActionOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
    await loadCrawlerTargets();
  } catch (e) { $('crActionOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function loadCrawlerPolicy() {
  try {
    const j = await api('/crawler/policy');
    $('crActionOut').textContent = 'Policy (' + j.count + ' states):\n' + JSON.stringify(j.policy.slice(0, 10), null, 2);
    if (j.policy.length > 0) {
      $('crBestQ').textContent = (j.policy[0].q_value || 0).toFixed(3);
    }
  } catch (e) { $('crActionOut').textContent = JSON.stringify(e, null, 2); }
}

async function loadCrawlerPage() {
  await loadCrawlerTargets();
  await loadCrawlerPolicy();
}

async function refreshAll() {
  await refreshStatus(); await searchMemory(); await loadReceipts(); await loadProcesses(); await loadApps(); await loadSettings();
}

// Auto refresh
refreshAll();
setInterval(refreshStatus, 15000);

              <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="addCell('app')">+ App</button>
              <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="runNotebook()">Save NB</button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="cells" id="cells">
            <div class="cell" data-kind="kernel">
              <div class="cell-gutter"><button class="cell-run" onclick="runCell(this)">▶</button>
                <div class="cell-num">1</div>
              </div>
              <div class="cell-body">
                <div class="cell-meta">Python / IPython</div>
